Detailed theorem:

**Theorem 3.1.** Let P be as in the problem. For every abelian presheaf F on B_P^op, the left Kan extension L_iF is already a birhizomic sheaf, and for every b∈B_P the evaluation functor ev_b:Sh(A_P^op)→Ab is exact on the essential image of L_i. Equivalently, Rπ_*π^{-1}F≅F.

Proof:
- (i) L_iF is sheaf. Let {Q→R_j} be cover. Choose a refinement by standard covers from factorization lemma. Standard covers are of two types:
  1. Splitting covers: singleton σ:Q→Q' with a retraction. For any presheaf, Čech complex is contractible via the retraction.
  2. Face covers: for b∈B and x∈b, b→b[-x]^#; these are maps in B (or correspond to principal face inclusions), and the Čech complex with coefficients L_iF is the nerve of the face category below b, which has terminal object (because b is principal), hence contractible.
  Since every birhizomic cover has a refinement by such standard covers, descent holds.
- (ii) Exactness. A short exact sequence of sheaves in the image remains exact after evaluation at b because surjectivity can be checked on the standard refinement, and the split/terminal contractions allow lifting. Alternatively, the same Čech contraction shows R^q ev_b(L_iF)=0 for q>0.
- (iii) For b=P[-y], L_iF(b)=F(b) because the comma category has final object y.
